Could simply take parent and apply a *deterministic improvement*: since parent is near optimal, we can perform a series of small coordinate transfers that reduce objective. The previous coordinate polish may not be strong enough. We can implement a more global local search:
- Compute c=FFT conv. Identify top K lags.
- For each coordinate i with large a_i, consider moving a fraction to coordinate j that minimizes a *linearized impact* on top lags: δF ≈ ∑_{top} λ_k ∂c_k/∂transfer. We can choose j with low score.
- Use exact evaluation for candidate moves (FFT each) but could be expensive. Instead precompute impact scores: moving δ from i to j changes c_k by δ( a_{k-j} - a_{k-i}) (if using c_k=∑ a_i a_{k-i}, and transfer preserves sum). For top K lags, define weight λ_k=max(0, c_k - m + ε) or all top. Impact I(i,j)=∑ λ_k (a_{k-j} - a_{k-i}). We want negative impact (reduce high lags) and not increase others. We can approximate with top K only. Then move small fraction from high a_i to low-impact j. Repeat. This is like coordinate descent with surrogate. Could be fast: precompute for each i a vector? We can compute impact scores for all j for a source i as convolution of weights with a. For weights w_k=λ_k, define u_j =∑_k w_k a_{k-j} (convolution of w and a). Then impact moving from i to j ≈ u_j - u_i. We want u_j small, u_i large. So pick source with high a_i*u_i? Specifically reducing a_i by δ and increasing j: δ(u_j - u_i). Choose pairs i,j with u_i > u_j and a_i>0. Move δ from high u_i to low u_j. This is a kind of *waterfilling* that reduces weighted peak. After many moves, recompute weights. This is multiplicative update: new a_i ∝ exp(-η u_i) maybe. Indeed mirror descent step a_i ← a_i exp(-η u_i)/sum. This is fast! It reduces the weighted sum ∑ w_k c_k. If w focus on current peaks, it may lower max. This is simpler than Nesterov and can use all top lags. We can implement adaptive multiplicative update with annealed η. This might improve parent.

Multiplicative algorithm details:
```
def entropy_update(a, n_top=20, q=2.0, eta0=0.05, n_iter=...):
    a=norm_sum1(a)
    best=a; best_m=conv.max()
    for it:
       c=fast_convolve(a)
       m=c.max()
       if m<best_m: best=a..., best_m=m
       if m < target_internal? maybe stop.
       # weights
       # use top K where K grows
       K = min(len(c), int(5 + it*0.5))
       idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]
       # window margin: include c > c[idx[-1]] - 0.2*m? maybe
       w = np.zeros(len(c))
       w[idx] = (c[idx] / (m+eps)) ** q  # all >0
       # Could also include all c > 0.5*m weighted
       w /= w.sum()
       # compute u = correlation as above
       u = corr_weights_a(w, a)
       # center u to avoid scale? update exp(-eta*(u - u.mean())) maybe preserves? If subtract mean, product changes? Since normalization removes constant factor, exp(-eta u) same as exp(-eta(u-mean)). Use centered to improve stability.
       uc = u - u.mean()
       # step size adaptive: eta = eta0 / sqrt(it+1) or constant; try multiple.
       for eta in [eta_current, eta*0.5, eta*2]:
           log_a = log(max(a,eps)) - eta*uc
           new=exp(log_a-log_a.max()); new/=sum
           c_new=fast_convolve(new); m_new=c_new.max()
           choose best eta
       a=best_new; eta adjust if improved.
```

def pnorm_update(a, p, eta, max_iter, time_limit, verbose=False):
    a=norm_sum1(a)
    best=a; best_m=score_internal(a)
    it=0; eta_cur=eta
    stall=0
    while time<limit and it<max_iter:
       c=fast_convolve(a); m=c.max(); it+=1
       if m < best_m - 1e-12: update best, stall=0; else stall+=1
       # weights
       c_pos = np.maximum(c, 1e-300)
       # To avoid underflow, log_w = (p-1)*(log(c_pos)-log(m))
       # Set only c > m*1e-3
       mask = c_pos > max(m*1e-3, 1e-300)
       log_w = np.full(len(c), -np.inf)
       log_w[mask] = (p-1)*(np.log(c_pos[mask])-np.log(m))
       # softmax to normalize weights? Since m factor, max log_w=0.
       w = np.zeros(len(c)); s=log_w[mask].max(); w[mask]=np.exp(log_w[mask]-s); w_norm=w.sum(); if w_norm>0: w/=w_norm
       u = corr_w_a(w, a) # using FFT
       # center u
       uc = u - u.mean()
       # step: try eta_cur, maybe adjust
       new = multiplicative_step(a, uc, eta_cur)
       c_new=fast_convolve(new); m_new=c_new.max()
       if m_new < m + 1e-15: # accept if not worse? maybe require best_m?
           a=new
           if m_new < best_m: best_m=m_new; best=a.copy(); stall=0
           # if improved a lot, eta*=1.1 else eta*=0.9
       else:
           eta_cur *= 0.5; if eta_cur<1e-6: break
       adaptive p? maybe p increases over time.
```

Good. So b_i=conv[K-i]. Great.

```
def pnorm_optimize(a0, time_limit, p_start=2.0, p_max=16.0, verbose=False):
    t0=time.monotonic(); a=norm_sum1(a0); c=fast_convolve(a); best_m=c.max(); best=a.copy()
    p=p_start; eta=0.0; stall=0; max_stall=800; it=0; rng=np.random.default_rng(123)
    while time...:
       it+=1
       c=fast_convolve(a); m=c.max()
       if m < best_m - 1e-12: best_m=m; best=a.copy(); stall=0; if it%100==0 print...
       else: stall+=1
       if stall > max_stall: # reheat
           p=min(p_max,p*1.5); eta=0; # perturb
           loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-30))+0.05*np.sqrt(p/2)*rng.standard_normal(len(a)); a=softmax; stall=0
           continue
       # weights
       c_safe=np.maximum(c,1e-300); m_safe=max(m,1e-300)
       logw=np.full(c.shape, -1e300)
       mask=c_safe > (m_safe*1e-4)
       if mask.any(): logw[mask]=(p-1.0)*np.log(c_safe[mask]/m_safe)
       s=logw[mask].max(); w=np.zeros_like(c); w[mask]=np.exp(logw[mask]-s); s=w.sum(); if s<=0: w[-1]=1 else w/=s
       u=corr_weights_a(w,a)
       uc=u-u.mean()
       usc=usp.std()
       if usc<1e-15: break/stall
       if eta < 1e-8: eta = 1.0/usc
       # trial step
       for factor in [1.0,0.5,2.0] maybe one? choose.
       new=exp(log(max(a,eps)) - eta*uc); new/=sum
       c_new=fast_convolve(new); m_new=c_new.max()
       if m_new < m:
           a=new
           if m_new < best_m: best_m=m_new; best=a.copy()
           eta *= 1.05
       else:
           # try smaller
           eta *=0.6
           if eta<1e-12: continue
           new=...
           c_new...
           if m_new < m: accept eta*? else stall
       # occasionally increase p if stall>100
       if stall>100: p=min(p_max,p*1.2)
    return best,best_m
```
Need avoid underflow in logw for large p. Use `np.where`. If p large, logw for c below m is negative large; exp underflows okay.

Step function top-level:
```
def exp_step(a, direction, eta):
   loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)) - eta*direction
   loga -= np.max(loga)
   b=np.exp(loga)
   b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
   s=b.sum()
   if s<1e-15: return a.copy()
   return b/s
```
Direction centered uc. Since constants drop, centering okay.

Detailed helper implementations:

`fast_convolve(a)`:
```
def fast_convolve(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0: return np.array([])
    size = 2*n - 1
    L = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)[:size]
    # numerical cleanup
    return c
```
Scaling: If sum not1, c sum=(sum)^2. For internal score assume sum1. `internal_score(a): a=norm_sum1(a); return 2*len(a)*fast_convolve(a).max()`.

`norm_sum1`:
```
a=np.maximum(np.asarray(a,float),0)
s=a.sum()
if s<1e-300: return np.ones_like(a)/len(a)
return a/s
```

`corr_weights_a(w,a)`:
```
n=len(a); K=2*n-2
w_rev = w[::-1]
size = len(w_rev)+n-1
L=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
W=np.fft.rfft(w_rev,L); A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
conv=np.fft.irfft(W*A, L)[:size]
# return b_i conv[K - i], i=0..n-1
return conv[K-n+1:K+1][::-1]? Let's index.
We need b[i]=conv[K-i] for i=0..n-1. That is conv[K],conv[K-1],...,conv[K-n+1]. So `out = conv[K-n+1:K+1][::-1]`.
Check: n=3,K=4, slice conv[2:5]=[b2,b1,b0], reverse [b0,b1,b2]. Good.
```
Use rfft. Ensure length K-n+1 positive: K=2n-2, K-n+1=n-1. slice from n-1 to K inclusive length n.

`pnorm_optimize`: Need manage time and p. We'll include a function `_make_weights(c, m, p)` top-level to avoid nested.
```
def make_pnorm_weights(c, m, p):
   L=len(c); w=np.zeros(L)
   if m <=0: w[-1]=1; return w
   mask = c > max(m*1e-4, 1e-300)
   if not np.any(mask): mask = np.ones(L,bool); # fallback
   logw = (p-1.0) * (np.log(c[mask]) - np.log(m))
   s = np.max(logw)
   vals = np.exp(logw - s)
   ssum = vals.sum()
   if ssum <=0: w[np.argmax(c)]=1; return w
   w[mask]=vals/ssum
   return w
```
If p is large, c/m <1 => logw negative; exp may underflow but okay.

In pnorm loop, after accepting/rejecting, maybe compute current `phi_p` for progress? not needed.
Reheating: add noise to log a:
```
loga = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-30))
loga += 0.02 * p ** 0.5 * rng.standard_normal(len(a))
a = exp softmax
```
If p large, maybe noise 0.05.

One concern: Multiplicative update with weights c^{p-1} and normalization may drive mass to coordinates where u is low, potentially making sequence spiky and increasing max. Line search on m prevents acceptance if max increases. But if all steps rejected at current eta, reducing eta may eventually accept tiny moves. If eta becomes too small, no improvement. Reheat.

We should also allow *increasing eta* if improved many times. If eta grows too large, trial may fail; adapt.

Maybe use a *plateau* strategy: even if m doesn't improve, accept a move if it reduces the p-norm `np.sum(c**p)` sufficiently. This can help navigate. We can compute `phi = np.sum(c**p)` (may overflow if p large; use log or p small). For p≤8, c~1/n, c^p tiny, sum okay. But if p=16, underflow. We can use pnorm only up to8. Accept if m_new <= m + small and phi_new < phi. Track best by m. This allows moves that don't immediately lower max but reshape. However, could drift away. Use with caution: accept if phi decreases and m does not increase by >0.5%? Revert if max worsens too much. We can implement:
```
phi = np.sum(c**p) if p<12 else 0
new_phi = np.sum(c_new**p)
if m_new < m: accept
elif m_new <= m*1.001 and new_phi < phi*0.999: accept
else reject
```
This may improve.

_LD_LOGBASE = None
_LD_BASIS = None
_LD_N = 0

def _log_polish_obj(x):
   if _LD_LOGBASE is None: return float('inf')
   logb = _LD_LOGBASE + _LD_BASIS.dot(x)
   logb = np.maximum(logb, -700)
   logb -= np.max(logb)
   b = np.exp(logb)
   b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
   s = b.sum()
   if s < 1e-300: return float('inf')
   b /= s
   return internal_score(b)
```

`coordinate_polish`: Could be optional. Let's implement a short version using pnorm gradient:
```
def coordinate_polish(a0, time_limit, rng_seed=7):
   a=norm_sum1(a0); best_m=fast_convolve(a).max(); c=fast_convolve(a)
   while time...:
      c=fast_convolve(a); m=c.max(); if m<best_m update
      # weights top
      top_k = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:20]
      # u from pnorm weights p=2
      w=make_pnorm_weights(c, c.max(), 2.0)
      u=corr_weights_a(w,a)
      # choose up to 20 source-dest pairs
      sources = np.argsort(a * u)[::-1][:30]
      dests = np.argsort(u)[:30]
      improved=False
      for s in sources[:10]:
         if a[s] <1e-12: continue
         for d_i in dests[:10]:
            if d_i==s: continue
            for frac in [0.002,0.005,0.01]:
               trial=a.copy(); amt=a[s]*frac; trial[s]-=amt; trial[d_i]+=amt; trial=np.maximum(trial,0); ssum=trial.sum(); trial/=ssum
               mt=fast_convolve(trial).max()
               if mt < best_m - 1e-14:
                  a=trial; best_m=mt; improved=True; break
            if improved: break
         if improved: break
      if not improved:
         stall++
         if stall > 100: # random move
            i=random source, j=random dest, frac small, if improves accept else break
   return best,best_m
```
This uses FFT per trial; max 300 per outer, each FFT. For n4096, 300*0.01s=3s per outer; too many. Limit time. Could use linearized acceptance: only evaluate every few? But okay if short 30s. We'll set time_limit maybe 60s.

Maybe skip coordinate polish if pnorm+LP enough.

   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   inits=[]
   if parent is not None: inits.append(interpolate_seq(parent,n))

pnorm loop pseudocode with acceptance:
```
def pnorm_optimize(a0, time_limit, p_start=2.0, p_max=8.0, verbose=False):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    a=norm_sum1(a0)
    c=fast_convolve(a); m=c.max(); best_m=m; best=a.copy()
    p=p_start; eta=0.0; stall=0; max_stall=600; it=0; rng=np.random.default_rng(55)
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
       it+=1
       c=fast_convolve(a); m=c.max()
       if m < best_m - 1e-13:
           best_m=m; best=a.copy(); stall=0
       else:
           stall+=1
       if stall > max_stall:
           # reheat
           p = min(p_max, p*1.5)
           eta=0.0
           loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-30))
           loga += 0.05*math.sqrt(max(1.0,p))*rng.standard_normal(len(a))
           loga -= np.max(loga)
           a=np.exp(loga); a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); a=a/s if s>0 else best.copy()
           stall=0
           if it%100==0 and verbose: print...
           continue
       if it % 200 ==0:
           p = min(p_max, p*1.05)
       w=make_pnorm_weights(c, m, p)
       u=corr_weights_a(w,a)
       uc=u - u.mean()
       rng_range=np.max(uc)-np.min(uc)
       if rng_range < 1e-15:
           # no gradient, try noise
           loga... noise; continue
       if eta < 1e-8:
           eta = 1.0 / rng_range
       # Try current eta, then 0.5
       accepted=False
       for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
           trial=exp_step(a, uc, eta*factor)
           ct=fast_convolve(trial); mt=ct.max()
           if mt < m - 1e-15 or (mt <= m*1.001 and mt < m): # first condition enough
               a=trial; eta*=1.05; accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
           eta *= 0.6
           if eta < 1e-10:
              # small noise
              loga += 0.01... ; continue
       # optional phi accept if mt equal? We already only accept if mt<m. Maybe too strict. Could allow if mt <= m+1e-12 and pnorm decreases.
```
Maybe accepting only if m decreases leads to many failures and slow. Let's include pnorm objective:
Compute current `phi` from c if p <= 10: `phi = np.sum(c**p)` (for p large, underflow okay; if underflow 0, use m maybe). For trial, compute phi_t. Accept if `mt < m` OR (`mt <= m + 0.2*(best_m-current?)?` no) AND `phi_t < phi * 0.999`. Since phi can be 0 due underflow, skip. For p=2, phi sum c^2 stable. We can accept if `mt <= m * 1.0005` and `phi_t < phi*0.999`. This allows movement that slightly raises max but reduces second moment, often useful. But could raise max above current; if it repeatedly increases, best_m unchanged. To prevent runaway, require `mt < m + 1e-6` absolute? m ~1e-5, 1e-6 too small. Use relative 1.001. We'll implement:

phi = None if p>10 else float(np.sum(c**p))
for factors:
  ...
  phi_t = None if p>10 else float(np.sum(ct**p))

```

_LD_LOGBASE = None
_LD_BASIS = None

def _ld_objective(x):
    if _LD_LOGBASE is None: return float('inf')
    logb = _LD_LOGBASE + _LD_BASIS.dot(x)
    logb = np.clip(logb, -700, 700)
    logb -= np.max(logb)
    b = np.exp(logb)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = b.sum()
    if s < 1e-300: return float('inf')
    b /= s
    return internal_score(b)

def low_dim_log_polish(a0, time_limit, verbose=False):
   if spo is None: return a0, internal_score(a0)
   n=len(a0); t0=time.monotonic()
   base=norm_sum1(a0); best_m=fast_convolve(base).max(); best=base.copy()
   xg=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   vecs=[]
   for j in range(1,7): v=xg**j; v-=v.mean(); vecs.append(v)
   for k in [1,2,3,4]:
      v=np.cos(2*np.pi*k*xg); v-=v.mean(); vecs.append(v)
      v=np.sin(2*np.pi*k*xg); v-=v.mean(); vecs.append(v)
   # normalize
   mat=np.array([v for v in vecs]).T
   norms=np.linalg.norm(mat, axis=0)
   good=norms>1e-12
   mat=mat[:,good]; mat[:,good]=mat[:,good]/norms[good]
   d=mat.shape[1]
   if d==0: return base,best_m
   global _LD_LOGBASE, _LD_BASIS
   _LD_LOGBASE=np.log(np.maximum(base,1e-30))
   _LD_LOGBASE -= _LD_LOGBASE.max()
   _LD_BASIS=mat
   rng=np.random.default_rng(3)
   starts=[np.zeros(d)]
   for _ in range(4): starts.append(rng.normal(0,0.1,d))
   for start in starts:
       if time.monotonic()-t0>time_limit: break
       try:
          res=spo.minimize(_ld_objective, start, method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':200, 'xatol':1e-4, 'fatol':1e-8, 'adapt':True})
          if res.x is not None:
             # evaluate res
             logb=...; b=...; sc=internal_score(b)
             if sc<best_m: best=b; best_m=sc
       except Exception: pass
   _LD_LOGBASE=None; _LD_BASIS=None
   return best,best_m
```

```
def exp_step(a, direction, eta):
    eps=1e-12/max(1,len(a))
    loga=np.log(np.maximum(a, eps)) - eta*direction
    loga-=np.max(loga)
    b=np.exp(loga)
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    s=b.sum()
    if s<=0: return a.copy()
    return b/s
```
This adds mass to zeros each step, okay.

def make_initial_candidates(n, parent=None):
    out=[]
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    if parent is not None:
       out.append(interpolate_seq(parent,n))
    out.append(norm_sum1(np.ones(n)))
    out.append(norm_sum1(x + 1e-12))
    out.append(norm_sum1((1.0-x) + 1e-12))
    out.append(norm_sum1(x**0.7 + 1e-12))
    out.append(norm_sum1(x**2.0 + 1e-12))
    out.append(norm_sum1(x*np.exp(0.5*x)))
    # triangular mode
    for cfrac in [0.25,0.4]:
        t=np.zeros(n); ci=int(round(cfrac*(n-1)))
        if ci>0: t[:ci+1]=np.linspace(0,1,ci+1)
        if ci<n-1: t[ci:]=np.linspace(1,0,n-ci)

    # random positive waves

    a=1+0.4*np.sin(2*np.pi*x+0.7)+0.2*rng.standard_normal(n); out.append(norm_sum1(a))
    return out

```python
"""
Hybrid search for small nonnegative-step-function auto-convolution peaks.

The objective is homogeneous, so the code mostly works with sum(a) = 1 and minimizes
  2*n*max(convolve(a,a)).

Improvements over the previous construction:
- fast p-norm multiplicative (entropy) updates using FFT-based gradients,
- CVXPY-based active-set Frank-Wolfe polish retained and retuned,
- low-dimensional log-linear polish around the best baseline,
- small coordinate/move polish,
- multi-length resize and controlled exploration from several initial shapes.
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np

try:
    import cvxpy as cp
except Exception:
    cp = None

try:
    from scipy import optimize as spo
except Exception:
    spo = None

_MODULE_SOLVER = None
_LD_LOGBASE = None
_LD_BASIS = None


def get_solver():
    global _MODULE_SOLVER
    if _MODULE_SOLVER is not None:
        return _MODULE_SOLVER
    if cp is None:
        return None
    for s in [cp.CLARABEL, cp.PDLP, cp.ECOS, cp.SCS]:
        try:
            x = cp.Variable(30, nonneg=True)
            prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cp.sum(x)), [cp.sum(x) == 1])
            prob.solve(solver=s, verbose=False)
            if prob.status in ("optimal", "optimal_inaccurate"):
                _MODULE_SOLVER = s
                print("  LP solver: " + str(s))
                return s
        except Exception:
            continue
    _MODULE_SOLVER = cp.ECOS
    print("  LP solver fallback: ECOS")
    return _MODULE_SOLVER


def fast_convolve(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:size]
    return c


def cross_convolve_fg(f, g):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(f)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    F = np.fft.rfft(f, L)
    G = np.fft.rfft(g, L)
    return np.fft.irfft(F * G, L)[:size]


def norm_sum1(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
        if len(a) == 0:
            return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
        return np.ones_like(a) / max(1, len(a))
    return a / s


def internal_score(a):
    a = norm_sum1(a)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = fast_convolve(a)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return 2.0 * float(n) * m


def evaluate_safe(a):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size == 0:
            return float("inf")
        val = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in arr])
        return float(val)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def interpolate_seq(f, new_n):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if f.size == 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, new_n), dtype=np.float64) / max(1, new_n)
    old_n = len(f)
    if old_n == new_n:
        return f.copy()
    s = f.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, new_n)
    fn = f / s
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    fi = np.interp(new_x, old_x, fn)
    fi = np.maximum(fi, 0.0)
    ss = fi.sum()
    if ss < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, new_n)
    return fi / ss


def get_initial_sequence():
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size > 0 and np.isfinite(p).all() and p.sum() > 1e-10:
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            p = p / p.sum()
            sc = internal_score(p)
            print(
                "Parent construction: n=" + str(len(p)) +
                " internal_score=" + repr(sc)
            )
            return p, sc
    except Exception:
        pass
    n = 2048
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = np.maximum(x ** 0.7, 0.0)
    a = a + 1e-12
    a = a / a.sum()
    sc = internal_score(a)
    print("Default initial: n=" + str(n) + " internal_score=" + repr(sc))
    return a, sc


def update_best(best_seq, best_score, a, label, t0, verify=False):
    if a is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return best_seq, best_score
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
        return best_seq, best_score
    a = a / s
    sc = internal_score(a)
    if not math.isfinite(sc):
        return best_seq, best_score
    if sc < best_score - 1e-9:
        if verify and a.size <= 6000:
            dsc = evaluate_safe(a)
            if math.isfinite(dsc):
                sc = dsc
        if sc < best_score:
            best_seq = a.copy()
            best_score = sc
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            print("*** NEW BEST [" + label + "]: " + str(sc) +
                  " (n=" + str(len(a)) + ", t=" + str(round(elapsed, 1)) + "s)")
    return best_seq, best_score


def exp_step(a, direction, eta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = max(1, len(a))
    eps = 1e-12 / n
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, eps)) - eta * direction
    maxlog = np.max(loga)
    loga = loga - maxlog
    b = np.exp(loga)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = b.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
        return norm_sum1(a)
    return b / s


def make_pnorm_weights(c, m, p):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.zeros_like(c)
    if m <= 1e-300:
        w[argmax_index(c)] = 1.0
        return w
    mask = c > max(m * 1e-4, 1e-300)
    if not np.any(mask):
        w[argmax_index(c)] = 1.0
        return w
    logw = (p - 1.0) * (np.log(np.maximum(c[mask], 1e-300)) - math.log(m))
    s = float(np.max(logw))
    vals = np.exp(logw - s)
    sm = float(vals.sum())
    if sm < 1e-300:
        w[argmax_index(c)] = 1.0
        return w
    w[mask] = vals / sm
    return w


def argmax_index(arr):
    return int(np.argmax(arr))


def corr_weights_a(w, a):
    """
    For w_k over linear conv lags k=0..2n-2 and a length n,
    returns u_i = sum_k w_k a_{k-i}, with a_j=0 outside [0,n-1].
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    K = 2 * n - 2
    w_rev = w[::-1]
    size = len(w_rev) + n - 1
    L = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    W = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(W * A, L)[:size]
    # b_i = conv[K - i]
    return conv[K - n + 1:K + 1][::-1].copy()


def pnorm_optimize(a0, time_limit, p_start=2.0, p_max=8.0, verbose=False):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    if len(a) == 0:
        return a, float("inf")
    c = fast_convolve(a)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    best = a.copy()
    best_m = m
    p = float(p_start)
    eta = 0.0
    stall = 0
    max_stall = 900
    it = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(777)

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        it += 1
        c = fast_convolve(a)
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        m = float(np.max(c))

        if m < best_m - 1e-13:
            best_m = m
            best = a.copy()
            stall = 0
            if verbose and it % 200 == 0:
                print("    pnorm it=" + str(it) + " score=" + repr(2.0 * len(a) * best_m) +
                      " p=" + repr(p))
        else:
            stall += 1

        if stall > max_stall:
            p = min(float(p_max), p * 1.5)
            eta = 0.0
            loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-14))
            noise_scale = 0.05 * math.sqrt(max(1.0, p * 0.5))
            loga += noise_scale * rng.standard_normal(len(a))
            loga -= float(np.max(loga))
            a = np.exp(loga)
            a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
            ss = a.sum()
            if ss < 1e-300:
                a = best.copy()
            else:
                a = a / ss
            stall = 0
            continue

        if it % 250 == 0:
            p = min(float(p_max), p * 1.05)

        w = make_pnorm_weights(c, m, p)
        u = corr_weights_a(w, a)
        uc = u - float(np.mean(u))
        rng_range = float(np.max(uc) - np.min(uc))
        if rng_range < 1e-15:
            loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-14))
            loga += 0.02 * rng.standard_normal(len(a))
            loga -= float(np.max(loga))
            a = np.exp(loga)
            a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
            ss = a.sum()
            a = a / ss if ss > 1e-300 else best.copy()
            continue

        if eta < 1e-8:
            eta = 1.0 / rng_range

        phi = None
        if p <= 10.0:
            try:
                phi = float(np.sum(c ** p))
            except Exception:
                phi = None

        accepted = False
        accepted_factor = 1.0
        for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
            trial = exp_step(a, uc, eta * factor)
            ct = fast_convolve(trial)
            ct = np.maximum(ct, 0.0)
            mt = float(np.max(ct))
            phi_t = None
            if p <= 10.0:
                try:
                    phi_t = float(np.sum(ct ** p))
                except Exception:
                    phi_t = None

            improve_m = mt < m - 1e-15
            improve_phi = (
                phi is not None and phi_t is not None and
                phi_t < phi * 0.999 and mt <= m * 1.001
            )
            if improve_m or improve_phi:
                a = trial
                accepted = True
                accepted_factor = factor
                if mt < best_m - 1e-14:
                    best_m = mt
                    best = a.copy()
                stall = 0
                break

        if accepted:
            if accepted_factor == 1.0:
                eta = min(1e8, eta * 1.03)
            else:
                eta = min(1e8, eta * 1.0)
        else:
            eta *= 0.6
            if eta < 1e-12:
                loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-14))
                loga += 0.01 * rng.standard_normal(len(a))
                loga -= float(np.max(loga))
                a = np.exp(loga)
                a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
                ss = a.sum()
                a = a / ss if ss > 1e-300 else best.copy()
                stall = 1
    return best, best_m


def build_constraint_matrix(y, constraint_list, n):
    K = len(constraint_list)
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    coeffs = np.zeros((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for jj in range(K):
        k_int = int(constraint_list[jj])
        i_start = max(0, k_int - n + 1)
        i_end = min(n, k_int + 1)
        if i_start < i_end:
            idx = np.arange(i_start, i_end)
            coeffs[jj, idx] = y[k_int - idx]
    return coeffs


def lp_fw(a0, time_limit, solver, initial_K=300, max_K=1200, add_per_iter=40, verbose=False):
    if cp is None or solver is None:
        return norm_sum1(a0), internal_score(a0)
    y = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = len(y)
    if n == 0:
        return y, float("inf")
    ac_y = np.maximum(fast_convolve(y), 0.0)
    best_a = y.copy()
    best_m = float(np.max(ac_y))
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    if len(ac_y) == 0:
        return best_a, best_m
    active_set = sorted(np.argsort(ac_y)[::-1][:initial_K].tolist())
    no_improve = 0
    max_no_improve = 700
    iter_count = 0
    stagnation_count = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        if no_improve > max_no_improve:
            if stagnation_count > 3:
                if verbose:
                    print("    LP-FW: persistent stagnation, stopping")
                break
            ac_curr = np.maximum(fast_convolve(y), 0.0)
            n_pert = max(40, len(active_set) // 5)
            keep = list(
                np.random.default_rng(123 + stagnation_count).choice(
                    len(active_set), size=max(1, len(active_set) - n_pert), replace=False
                )
            )
            new_adds = np.argsort(ac_curr)[::-1][:max(1, 2 * n_pert)]
            new_set = set([active_set[i] for i in keep])
            for k in new_adds:
                new_set.add(int(k))
            active_set = sorted(new_set)
            no_improve = 0
            stagnation_count += 1
            continue

        iter_count += 1
        ac = np.maximum(fast_convolve(y), 0.0)
        M_curr = float(np.max(ac))

        if M_curr < best_m - 1e-14:
            best_m = M_curr
            best_a = y.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            stagnation_count = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if len(active_set) > max_K:
            margins = np.array([M_curr - float(ac[k]) for k in active_set])
            keep_idx = np.argsort(margins)[:int(max_K * 0.7)]
            active_set_base = [active_set[i] for i in keep_idx]
            violations = np.where(ac > M_curr - 1e-9)[0]
            for v in violations[:100]:
                iv = int(v)
                if iv not in active_set_base:
                    active_set_base.append(iv)
            active_set = sorted(set(active_set_base))

        K = len(active_set)
        if K == 0:
            break
        if K > max_K:
            active_set = active_set[:max_K]
            K = max_K

        try:
            coeffs = build_constraint_matrix(y, active_set, n)
        except Exception:
            active_set = active_set[:max(50, K // 3)]
            continue

        g_var = cp.Variable(n, nonneg=True)
        M_var = cp.Variable()
        constraints = [cp.sum(g_var) == 1, coeffs @ g_var <= M_var]
        prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(M_var), constraints)

        try:
            prob.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)
        except Exception:
            active_set = active_set[:max(50, K // 3)]
            continue

        if prob.status not in ("optimal", "optimal_inaccurate"):
            continue

        try:
            g_val = np.maximum(np.asarray(g_var.value, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
        except Exception:
            continue
        gs = g_val.sum()
        if gs < 1e-300:
            continue
        g_dir = g_val / gs

        conv_ys = np.maximum(cross_convolve_fg(y, g_dir), 0.0)
        all_viol = np.where(conv_ys > M_curr + 1e-10)[0]
        if len(all_viol) > 0:
            viol_amt = conv_ys[all_viol] - M_curr
            top_v = all_viol[np.argsort(viol_amt)[::-1][:add_per_iter]]
            new_set = set(active_set)
            for k in top_v:
                new_set.add(int(k))
            active_set = sorted(new_set)[:max_K]

        best_alpha = max(0.05, 1.0 / (iter_count + 2.0))
        best_trial_M = M_curr

        alphas_to_try = [
            1.0,
            0.5,
            max(0.08, 0.4 / (iter_count + 1.0)),
            max(0.02, 0.12 / (iter_count + 1.0)),
        ]

        for alpha in alphas_to_try:
            trial = (1.0 - alpha) * y + alpha * g_dir
            trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
            st = trial.sum()
            if st < 1e-300:
                continue
            trial = trial / st
            Mt = float(np.max(np.maximum(fast_convolve(trial), 0.0)))
            if Mt < best_trial_M:
                best_trial_M = Mt
                best_alpha = alpha

        if iter_count <= 2:
            y = g_dir.copy()
        else:
            y = (1.0 - best_alpha) * y + best_alpha * g_dir
            y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
            sy = y.sum()
            if sy < 1e-300:
                y = best_a.copy()
            else:
                y = y / sy

        if verbose and iter_count % 120 == 0:
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            print(
                "    LP-FW iter=" + str(iter_count) +
                " (" + str(round(elapsed, 1)) + "s): score=" + repr(2.0 * n * best_m) +
                " |S|=" + str(len(active_set))
            )

    if verbose:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        print("    LP-FW done (" + str(round(elapsed, 1)) + "s): score=" +
              repr(2.0 * n * best_m) + " iters=" + str(iter_count))
    return best_a, best_m


def _ld_objective(x):
    if _LD_LOGBASE is None or _LD_BASIS is None:
        return float("inf")
    try:
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        logb = _LD_LOGBASE + _LD_BASIS.dot(x)
        logb = np.clip(logb, -700.0, 700.0)
        logb -= float(np.max(logb))
        b = np.exp(logb)
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        s = b.sum()
        if s < 1e-300:
            return float("inf")
        b = b / s
        return internal_score(b)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def low_dim_log_polish(a0, time_limit, verbose=False):
    if spo is None:
        a = norm_sum1(a0)
        return a, internal_score(a)

    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return a, float("inf")
    best = a.copy()
    best_m = internal_score(a)
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    xg = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    vecs = []
    for j in range(1, 7):
        v = xg ** float(j)
        v -= float(np.mean(v))
        vecs.append(v)
    for kk in (1, 2, 3, 4):
        v = np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(kk) * xg)
        v -= float(np.mean(v))
        vecs.append(v)
        v = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(kk) * xg)
        v -= float(np.mean(v))
        vecs.append(v)

    if len(vecs) == 0:
        return best, best_m

    mat = np.array(vecs, dtype=np.float64).T
    norms = np.linalg.norm(mat, axis=0)
    good = norms > 1e-12
    if not np.any(good):
        return best, best_m
    mat = mat[:, good]
    mat = mat / norms[good]

    d = mat.shape[1]
    global _LD_LOGBASE, _LD_BASIS
    logbase = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-14))
    logbase -= float(np.max(logbase))
    _LD_LOGBASE = logbase
    _LD_BASIS = mat

    rng = np.random.default_rng(13)
    starts = [np.zeros(d)]
    for _ in range(5):
        starts.append(0.08 * rng.standard_normal(d))

    try:
        for st in starts:
            if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit:
                break
            try:
                res = spo.minimize(
                    _ld_objective,
                    st,
                    method="Nelder-Mead",
                    options={
                        "maxiter": 180,
                        "xatol": 1e-4,
                        "fatol": 1e-9,
                        "adaptive": True,
                    },
                )
                if res.x is not None:
                    sc = _ld_objective(res.x)
                    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_m - 1e-12:
                        best_m = sc
                        best = _ld_x_to_seq(res.x)
                        if verbose:
                            print("    low-dim polish score=" + repr(best_m))
            except Exception:
                pass
    finally:
        _LD_LOGBASE = None
        _LD_BASIS = None

    return best, best_m


def _ld_x_to_seq(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if _LD_LOGBASE is None or _LD_BASIS is None:
        return None
    logb = _LD_LOGBASE + _LD_BASIS.dot(x)
    logb = np.clip(logb, -700.0, 700.0)
    logb -= float(np.max(logb))
    b = np.exp(logb)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = b.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
        return None
    return b / s


def coordinate_polish(a0, time_limit, rng_seed=99, verbose=False):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return a, float("inf")
    best = a.copy()
    best_m = internal_score(a)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(rng_seed)
    stall = 0
    max_stall = 300
    step_fracs = (0.002, 0.005, 0.01, 0.02)

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit and stall < max_stall:
        c = np.maximum(fast_convolve(a), 0.0)
        m = float(np.max(c))
        if m < best_m - 1e-14:
            best_m = m
            best = a.copy()
            stall = 0

        top = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:min(24, len(c))]
        w = make_pnorm_weights(c, m, 2.0)
        u = corr_weights_a(w, a)

        sources = np.argsort(a * u)[::-1][:min(24, n)]
        dests = np.argsort(u)[:min(24, n)]
        improved = False

        for s_idx in sources[:12]:
            si = int(s_idx)
            if a[si] < 1e-12:
                continue
            for d_idx in dests[:12]:
                di = int(d_idx)
                if di == si:
                    continue
                for frac in step_fracs:
                    amt = a[si] * frac
                    if amt < 1e-16:
                        continue
                    trial = a.copy()
                    trial[si] -= amt
                    trial[di] += amt
                    trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
                    ts = trial.sum()
                    if ts < 1e-300:
                        continue
                    trial = trial / ts
                    mt = float(np.max(np.maximum(fast_convolve(trial), 0.0)))
                    if mt < best_m - 1e-15:
                        a = trial
                        best_m = mt
                        best = a.copy()
                        improved = True
                        break
                if improved:
                    break
            if improved:
                if verbose and stall % 200 == 0:
                    print("    coord polish t=" + str(round(time.monotonic() - t0, 1)) +
                          " score=" + repr(2.0 * n * best_m))
                stall = 0
                break

        if not improved:
            stall += 1
            if stall % 80 == 0:
                for _ in range(8):
                    si = int(rng.integers(n))
                    di = int(rng.integers(n))
                    if si == di or a[si] < 1e-12:
                        continue
                    frac = float(rng.uniform(0.002, 0.015))
                    amt = a[si] * frac
                    trial = a.copy()
                    trial[si] -= amt
                    trial[di] += amt
                    trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
                    ts = trial.sum()
                    if ts < 1e-300:
                        continue
                    trial = trial / ts
                    mt = float(np.max(np.maximum(fast_convolve(trial), 0.0)))
                    if mt < best_m - 1e-15:
                        a = trial
                        best_m = mt
                        best = a.copy()
                        stall = 0
                        break
        if verbose and stall > 0 and stall % 500 == 0:
            print("    coord stall=" + str(stall) + " score=" + repr(2.0 * n * best_m))

    return best, best_m


def make_initial_candidates(n, parent=None):
    out = []
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    if parent is not None:
        out.append(interpolate_seq(parent, n))

    out.append(norm_sum1(np.ones(n)))
    out.append(norm_sum1(x + 1e-12))
    out.append(norm_sum1((1.0 - x) + 1e-12))
    out.append(norm_sum1(x ** 0.7 + 1e-12))
    out.append(norm_sum1(x ** 2.0 + 1e-12))
    out.append(norm_sum1(x * np.exp(0.5 * x)))

    for cfrac in (0.25, 0.40):
        t = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        ci = int(round(cfrac * (n - 1)))
        ci = min(max(ci, 1), n - 1)
        if ci > 0:
            t[:ci + 1] = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, ci + 1)
        t[ci:] = np.linspace(1.0, 0.0, n - ci)
        out.append(norm_sum1(t))

    rng = np.random.default_rng(17)
    a = 1.0 + 0.4 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x + 0.7) + 0.18 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    out.append(norm_sum1(a))

    return out


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t_global = time.monotonic()
    time_budget = max(60.0, min(float(budget_s) - 90.0, 1710.0))
    print("Search time budget: " + repr(time_budget) + "s")

    parent_seq, parent_score = get_initial_sequence()

    # Keep total output length reasonable for the evaluator's direct convolution.
    MAX_OUTPUT_N = 8192
    if len(parent_seq) > MAX_OUTPUT_N:
        parent_work = interpolate_seq(parent_seq, 6000)
    else:
        parent_work = parent_seq.copy()

    best_seq, best_score = parent_work, internal_score(parent_work)
    if len(best_seq) <= 6000:
        dsc = evaluate_safe(best_seq)
        if math.isfinite(dsc):
            best_score = dsc
    print("Baseline score: " + repr(best_score) + " n=" + str(len(best_seq)))

    solver = get_solver()

    def remaining():
        return time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_global)

    # ---------------- Phase 1: entropy/p-norm optimization from parent ----------------
    if remaining() > 240:
        t1 = min(remaining() * 0.38, 1300.0)
        # Choose a working length for the main optimizer.
        if 512 <= len(best_seq) <= 8192:
            main_n = len(best_seq)
        elif len(best_seq) > 8192:
            main_n = 5000
        else:
            main_n = 2048
        a_main = interpolate_seq(best_seq, main_n)
        print("\n=== Phase 1: p-norm multiplicative (n=" + str(main_n) + ") ===")
        a_out, _ = pnorm_optimize(a_main, t1, p_start=2.0, p_max=8.0, verbose=True)
        best_seq, best_score = update_best(
            best_seq, best_score, a_out, "pnorm_main", t_global, verify=True
        )

    # ---------------- Phase 2: LP-FW polish ----------------
    if remaining() > 240 and solver is not None and 512 <= len(best_seq) <= 7000:
        t2 = min(remaining() * 0.28, 700.0)
        print("\n=== Phase 2: LP-FW (n=" + str(len(best_seq)) + ") ===")
        a_out, _ = lp_fw(
            norm_sum1(best_seq),
            t2,
            solver,
            initial_K=250,
            max_K=1000,
            add_per_iter=35,
            verbose=True,
        )
        best_seq, best_score = update_best(
            best_seq, best_score, a_out, "lpfw1", t_global, verify=True
        )

    # ---------------- Phase 3: second p-norm pass ----------------
    if remaining() > 160:
        t3 = min(remaining() * 0.22, 450.0)
        print("\n=== Phase 3: p-norm pass 2 (n=" + str(len(best_seq)) + ") ===")
        a_out, _ = pnorm_optimize(
            norm_sum1(best_seq), t3, p_start=3.0, p_max=8.0, verbose=False
        )
        best_seq, best_score = update_best(
            best_seq, best_score, a_out, "pnorm2", t_global, verify=True
        )

    # ---------------- Phase 4: multi-length exploration ----------------
    if remaining() > 260:
        print("\n=== Phase 4: multi-length exploration ===")
        base_n = len(best_seq)
        try_ns = sorted(
            set(
                [base_n]
                + [
                    max(1024, base_n - 1000),
                    max(1024, base_n + 1000),
                    2048,
                    4096,
                    8192,
                ]
            )
        )
        try_ns = [nn for nn in try_ns if 1024 <= nn <= 8192]
        done = 0
        for nn in try_ns[:6]:
            if remaining() < 120 or done > 4:
                break
            if nn == base_n:
                continue
            a_init = interpolate_seq(best_seq, nn)
            t4 = min(remaining() * 0.14, 180.0)
            if t4 < 35.0:
                break
            print("  exploring n=" + str(nn) + " remaining=" + str(round(remaining(), 1)))
            a_out, _ = pnorm_optimize(a_init, t4, p_start=2.5, p_max=8.0, verbose=False)
            best_seq, best_score = update_best(
                best_seq, best_score, a_out, "pnorm_n" + str(nn), t_global, verify=True
            )
            done += 1

    # ---------------- Phase 5: low-dimensional log polish ----------------
    if remaining() > 90 and len(best_seq) <= 8192:
        t5 = min(remaining() * 0.14, 160.0)
        if t5 > 30.0:
            print("\n=== Phase 5: low-dim log polish (n=" + str(len(best_seq)) + ") ===")
            a_out, _ = low_dim_log_polish(norm_sum1(best_seq), t5, verbose=True)
            best_seq, best_score = update_best(
                best_seq, best_score, a_out, "lowdim", t_global, verify=True
            )

    # ---------------- Phase 6: coordinate polish ----------------
    if remaining() > 80:
        t6 = min(remaining() * 0.16, 200.0)
        if t6 > 25.0:
            print("\n=== Phase 6: coordinate polish (n=" + str(len(best_seq)) + ") ===")
            a_out, _ = coordinate_polish(norm_sum1(best_seq), t6, rng_seed=2024, verbose=True)
            best_seq, best_score = update_best(
                best_seq, best_score, a_out, "coord", t_global, verify=True
            )

    # ---------------- Phase 7: final LP polish if time permits ----------------
    if remaining() > 120 and solver is not None and 512 <= len(best_seq) <= 7000:
        t7 = min(remaining() * 0.32, 360.0)
        print("\n=== Phase 7: final LP-FW (n=" + str(len(best_seq)) + ") ===")
        a_out, _ = lp_fw(
            norm_sum1(best_seq),
            t7,
            solver,
            initial_K=350,
            max_K=1200,
            add_per_iter=50,
            verbose=True,
        )
        best_seq, best_score = update_best(
            best_seq, best_score, a_out, "lpfw_final", t_global, verify=True
        )

    # ---------------- Optional: exploration from scratch if still not enough ----------------
    if remaining() > 300 and best_score > 1.5027:
        print("\n=== Phase 8: exploration from alternative initializations ===")
        for nn in (2048, 4096):
            if remaining() < 180:
                break
            inits = make_initial_candidates(nn, best_seq)
            # Limit to a few cheap runs.
            for idx in range(min(4, len(inits))):
                if remaining() < 80:
                    break
                t8 = min(remaining() * 0.10, 60.0)
                if t8 < 25.0:
                    break
                a_out, _ = pnorm_optimize(inits[idx], t8, p_start=2.0, p_max=8.0, verbose=False)
                best_seq, best_score = update_best(
                    best_seq,
                    best_score,
                    a_out,
                    "explore_n" + str(nn) + "_i" + str(idx),
                    t_global,
                    verify=False,
                )

    # ---------------- Final short polish if possible ----------------
    if remaining() > 60:
        t9 = min(remaining() * 0.55, 180.0)
        print("\n=== Phase 9: final entropy polish ===")
        a_out, _ = pnorm_optimize(norm_sum1(best_seq), t9, p_start=4.0, p_max=8.0, verbose=True)
        best_seq, best_score = update_best(
            best_seq, best_score, a_out, "final_pnorm", t_global, verify=True
        )

    # ---------------- Final deterministic checks ----------------
    best_seq = norm_sum1(best_seq)
    if len(best_seq) > MAX_OUTPUT_N:
        best_seq = interpolate_seq(best_seq, MAX_OUTPUT_N)

    final_direct = evaluate_safe(best_seq)
    if math.isfinite(final_direct):
        best_score = final_direct
    else:
        # Fallback to the original parent if it is safely sized, otherwise uniform.
        try:
            if parent_seq is not None and len(parent_seq) <= MAX_OUTPUT_N:
                fallback = norm_sum1(parent_seq)
            else:
                nfb = 2048
                x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, nfb)
                fallback = norm_sum1(x ** 0.7 + 1e-12)
        except Exception:
            nfb = 1024
            x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, nfb)
            fallback = norm_sum1(x)
        fs = evaluate_safe(fallback)
        if math.isfinite(fs):
            best_seq = fallback
            best_score = fs
            final_direct = fs
        else:
            best_seq = norm_sum1(np.ones(512))
            best_score = evaluate_safe(best_seq)
            final_direct = best_score

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_global
    print(
        "FINAL reported score: " + str(best_score) +
        " direct=" + repr(final_direct) +
        " n=" + str(len(best_seq)) +
        " elapsed=" + str(round(elapsed, 1)) + "s"
    )

    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```
